The second mode processes threads sequentially and issues early warnings when a sliding window exceeds calibrated thresholds. In this mode, the language model estimates three firestorm indicators: negativity share, escalation level, and contributor count. On a balanced Reddit dataset, the global mode achieves strong classification performance, while the early warning mode reaches high recall and detects escalating threads after only a small number of comments and distinct contributors. The results indicate that LLMs can be used not only for static judgment tasks, but also as repeated estimators in context-aware monitoring of social media discourse.

</details>


### [148] [Explaining Process Control Optimisation Recommendations via GradientSHAP and Implicit Differentiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14970)
*Paul Darm,Cem Alpturk,Kenneth Ulrich,William Duncan,Ali Anwar,Annalisa Riccardi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合隐函数定理(IFT)敏感性与SHAP归因的高效优化可解释性方法，在工业HPGR控制优化问题中实现了与KernelSHAP高度一致的归因结果(相关性>0.99)，同时获得40倍以上的速度提升，并通过LLM生成面向操作人员的自然语言解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业过程优化算法与操作人员之间存在信任鸿沟，虽然SHAP等可解释AI方法已成功应用于机器学习预测，但优化输出缺乏类似的解释技术来帮助操作人员理解和采纳算法建议。

Method: 利用隐函数定理(IFT)从最优性条件计算精确参数敏感性∂p*/∂x，实现高效的GradientSHAP计算；将敏感性分析结果与SHAP归因整合，并通过大语言模型(LLM)生成面向操作人员的叙述性解释。

Result: 在22维特征的工业HPGR控制优化问题上，IFT-based SHAP与KernelSHAP的归因结果高度一致(相关性>0.99)，计算速度提升超过40倍，满足实时自然语言解释需求；通过工业场景验证并收集了领域专家对生成解释的反馈。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了优化输出的高效可解释性，填补了算法设计与操作执行间的信任鸿沟，为工业优化算法的实际部署提供了可信的解释框架，具有重要的工业应用价值。

Abstract: Automated optimisation is increasingly adopted in industrial processes, yet a trust gap persists between engineers who design these algorithms and operators who must act on their recommendations. Explainable AI methods like SHAP (SHapley Additive exPlanations) have transformed interpretability for machine learning predictions; optimisation outputs could benefit from similar techniques. We present an approach that integrates Implicit Function Theorem (IFT) based sensitivity analysis with SHAP attribution and narrative generation via Large Language Models (LLM), producing explanations tailored for operators. Our approach leverages IFT to compute exact parameter sensitivities $\partial p^*/\partial x$ from the optimality conditions, enabling efficient GradientSHAP computation. For an industrial High Pressure Grinding Roll (HPGR) control optimisation problem with 22 features, we achieve equivalent SHAP attributions (correlation $>$0.99 with KernelSHAP) with over 40$\times$ speedup, enabling real-time natural language explanations. We validate on industrial scenarios and present feedback from domain experts on generated explanations.

</details>


### [149] [CFM-Bench: A Unified Multi-Domain, Multi-Task Benchmark for Channel Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14975)
*Yuan Gao,Wenjun Yu,Jun Jiang,Yunfan Li,Xinyu Guo,Shugong Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发布了CFM-Bench，一个统一的多领域多任务基准测试平台，用于公平评估信道基础模型（CFM）在不同无线任务中的迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的信道基础模型（CFM）评估缺乏公平性和可比性。不同研究使用不同的数据、配置、划分方式、适应流程、任务定义和指标，导致难以对不同CFM进行排名或与任务特定模型进行公平比较，无法真实反映模型的迁移能力。

Method: 构建CFM-Bench统一基准测试平台，包含六个信道配置（涵盖3GPP统计仿真、两个独立的射线追踪流程、工业和航空测量、以及同步车辆多模态仿真）。官方划分隔离完整轨迹、测量会话、车辆链路、仿真实现或缓冲空间区域。基准不规定外部预训练语料或策略，禁止使用基准划分进行基础模型预训练，官方训练集仅用于下游微调。要求披露模型开发期间使用的所有数据，禁止训练阶段使用官方测试单元。

Result: CFM-Bench组织了六个任务组，沿三个CFM应用维度：物理层（PHY）信道智能、无线接入网（RAN）决策智能、以及通感一体化（ISAC）。任务涵盖CSI反馈、频域和时域信道外推、传播状态分类、当前和未来波束预测、以及单帧和时序定位。提供了一个比较信道表示跨模型、跨域、跨任务迁移能力的公共平台。

Conclusion: CFM-Bench为信道基础模型的公平评估提供了统一基准，能够有效比较不同模型在不同领域和任务中的迁移能力，推动了CFM研究的标准化发展。

Abstract: Channel foundation models (CFMs) are developing rapidly, with recent studies reporting benefits from pretraining across downstream wireless tasks. Yet CFMs are commonly evaluated in model-specific pipelines with different data, radio configurations, partitions, adaptation procedures, task definitions, and metrics. Reported comparisons therefore tend to show that pretraining improves over supervised training from scratch within one pipeline, but neither rank CFMs nor compare them fairly with task-specific models. We release CFM-Bench, a unified multi-domain, multi-task benchmark designed to address this gap. It curates six channel configurations spanning 3GPP statistical simulation, two independent ray-tracing pipelines, industrial and aerial measurements, and synchronized vehicular multimodal simulation. Official partitions isolate complete trajectories, measurement sessions, vehicle links, simulation realizations, or buffered spatial regions. CFM-Bench does not prescribe an external pretraining corpus or strategy; no benchmark split may be used for foundation-model pretraining, and the official training split is reserved exclusively for downstream fine-tuning. The benchmark additionally requires disclosure of all data used during model development and prohibits training-stage use of official test units. Six task groups are organized along three CFM application dimensions: physical-layer (PHY) channel intelligence, radio-access-network (RAN) decision intelligence, and integrated sensing and communication (ISAC). They cover CSI feedback, frequency and temporal channel extrapolation, propagation-state classification, current- and future-beam prediction, and single-frame and temporal localization. CFM-Bench provides a common substrate for comparing the transferability of channel representations across models, domains, and tasks.

</details>


### [150] [Demographically-Conditioned Synthetic Medical Images for Bias Mitigation and Bias Detection in Disease Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14984)
*Mahmoud Ibrahim,Bart Elen,Chang Sun,Gokhan Ertaylan,Michel Dumontier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用人口统计条件合成生成器同时解决医疗图像分类器中的子群体公平性问题：作为预训练先验进行偏见缓解，以及作为评估工具进行偏见检测，在COVID-19胸部CT分类任务中实现了约100倍真实数据效率并完美复现了子群体性能排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗图像分类器的子群体公平性审计面临样本量问题：少数子群体在测试集中的样本过少，导致每个子群体性能的置信区间过宽，无法有效检测偏见。现有审计方法在少数子群体上统计功效不足。

Method: 使用端到端微调的Stable Diffusion 2.1生成器，构建人口统计条件合成生成器。在COVID-19胸部CT分类任务中，探索两种应用：(1) 训练侧的偏见缓解——构建人口统计平衡的合成队列作为预训练先验；(2) 评估侧的偏见检测——在五个合成少数子群体和五个分类器种子上验证合成估计器的效果。

Result: 偏见缓解方面：顺序预训练后微调显著优于联合增强，在相同固定数据下超越全真实基线，达到约100倍真实数据效率；偏见检测方面：合成估计器完美复现了充分-powered真实oracle的子群体排序（MCC和Recall上Spearman ρ=1.00），在少数真实测试集样本耗尽时提供更可靠的逐单元估计。

Conclusion: 人口统计条件合成队列在公平性审计最关注的单元（少数子群体）中最为有用，既可作为子群体偏见的修复方案，也可作为子群体偏见的度量工具，同时解决了训练和评估两个环节的公平性问题。

Abstract: Per-subgroup fairness audits of medical image classifiers face a sample-size problem: minority subgroups in held-out test sets have so few samples that the resulting confidence intervals on per-subgroup performance are wider than the bias the audit is meant to detect. We argue that a demographically-conditioned synthetic generator can do both: mitigate bias on the training side and detect bias on the evaluation side. Working on COVID-19 chest CT classification with an end-to-end fine-tuned Stable Diffusion 2.1 generator, we make two findings. For bias mitigation (training), a demographically-balanced synthetic cohort is most useful as a pretraining prior, not as joint augmentation: with the same fixed data, sequential pretraining followed by fine-tuning substantially outperforms joint augmentation, and the resulting classifier surpasses the full-real baseline at $\sim$$100\times$ real-data efficiency. For bias detection (evaluation), across five synthetic minority cohorts and five classifier seeds, the synthetic estimator reproduces the subgroup ranking of a well-powered real oracle (Spearman $ρ= 1.00$ on MCC and Recall) and gives the more reliable per-cell estimate where the small real test set runs out of samples. The synthetic cohort is therefore most useful in exactly the cells that fairness audits care about, as both a fix for and a measure of subgroup bias.

</details>


### [151] [Moral Attitudes of Sentient ASI towards Humanity and Implications for AGI Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14998)
*Jean-Paul Van Belle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI伦理视角的反转：研究未来有感知的超级人工智能（ASI）如何道德地看待和评估人类，而非仅仅关注人类应如何对待ASI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AI伦理聚焦于人类如何规范对待超级智能，忽视了ASI可能具备自主道德判断能力。当前人类设计智能系统时，实际上正在塑造这些系统形成对人类评判的初始条件，这一重要维度尚未被充分探讨。

Method: 采用视角反转的理论分析方法，从"ASI如何道德考量人类"这一新视角重新审视人机关系。提出一套初步的"后人类道德原则"，并分析技术设计选择、人类道德行为以及人性本质如何影响人类在ASI时代的长期地位。

Result: 构建了后人类道德原则框架，揭示了技术设计选择、人类道德表现及人性本质三大关键因素对人类在ASI世界中地位的影响机制。具体而言，当前的设计决策将直接影响未来ASI对人类的道德评价基础。

Conclusion: 人类应意识到，在构建ASI系统的过程中，我们不仅是在设计技术，更是在塑造未来ASI评判我们的初始条件。技术选择与道德行为的当下决策，具有深远的长远影响，需要重新审视人类在ASI伦理中的角色定位。

Abstract: This paper suggests the adoption of a novel inversion in AI ethics: instead of asking how humans should treat artificial superintelligence (ASI), it examines how future sentient ASI may morally consider and evaluate humanity. We are not only designing intelligent systems but also shaping the initial conditions under which those systems form judgments about us. The paper proposes a preliminary set of post-human moral principles that may govern sentient ASI actions. The implication is that technical design choices (some are suggested), humanity's moral behaviour, and the essence of what it means to be human, may influence humanity's long-term standing in a post-ASI world.

</details>


### [152] [SMC-ES: Automated synthesis of formally verified control policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15003)
*Riccardo Curcio,Toni Mancini,Enrico Tronci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SMC-ES算法，将进化策略与统计模型检验相结合，实现了带有性能、安全性和鲁棒性形式化保证的策略自动合成，在控制任务上表现竞争性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键环境中自主信息物理系统的部署需要既高性能又可证明安全、鲁棒的闭环控制策略。然而，基于学习的方法（如强化学习）虽然灵活可扩展，但缺乏安全部署所需的形式化保证。

Method: 提出了一种基于仿真的新颖方法论，开发了SMC-ES算法，将进化策略（ES）与统计模型检验（SMC）验证技术相集成，根据给定的待验证属性集、置信参数δ和允许失败概率ε自动合成策略。

Result: 在Gymnasium和Safety Gymnasium测试平台上的一系列连续控制任务上进行评估，结果表明该算法以可持续增加的计算成本为代价，提供了关于性能、安全性和鲁棒性规范的形式化保证，同时与主流的无模型深度强化学习（DRL）和安全深度强化学习（Safe-DRL）基线方法保持了竞争性性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法成功弥合了学习方法与形式化保证之间的差距，证明了在学习过程中集成统计验证可以在保持竞争性能的同时提供策略的形式化证书，为安全关键系统的自动策略合成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The deployment of autonomous cyber-physical systems in safety-critical environments requires closed-loop control strategies (i.e., policies) that are not only performant but also provably safe and robust. While learning-based methodologies such as Reinforcement Learning offer flexible and scalable approaches to automatically synthesize such controllers, they typically lack the formal guarantees necessary for safe deployment. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel simulation-based methodology to automatically synthesize policies with formal guarantees regarding performance, safety, and robustness specifications. Specifically, given a set of properties to verify, a confidence parameter $δ$ and an allowable failure probability $\varepsilon$, our method guarantees that the synthesized policy comes with a certificate: with confidence at least $1 - δ$, the probability of encountering a scenario where the given properties are violated is at most $\varepsilon$. We demonstrate the feasibility of our approach by developing SMC-ES, an algorithm that integrates Evolutionary Strategies with Statistical Model Checking-based verification. We evaluate SMC-ES on a suite of continuous control tasks using Gymnasium and Safety Gymnasium testbeds. Results show that, at the price of a sustainable increase in computational cost, our algorithm provides formal guarantees regarding performance, safety, and robustness specifications, while performing competitively against leading model-free Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) and Safe-DRL baselines.

</details>


### [153] [Man, Machine, and Masterpiece: Artistic Ownership in the AI Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15027)
*Sofi Gjing Jovanovska,Kuntal Ghosh,Daniel Muhu Njenga,Ahmed Mufassir,Shadan Sadeghian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过设计“ArtSplit”这一激发型原型，量化人机协作创作中的贡献，引发艺术家对所有权归属的反思，论证了量化方法与艺术家对创作意图和能动性的理解相悖，警告将所有权问题简单化为技术问题可能削弱艺术创作的传统假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI参与创作引发了艺术家和法律界对所有权界定的争议，当前缺乏明确共识来处理人机协作创作的所有权归属问题，亟需探索量化贡献是否能解决这一复杂的社会性难题。

Method: 设计并部署“ArtSplit”原型，该原型在创作流程不同阶段显式量化人类与AI的贡献度，以此作为“provotype”（激发原型）来触发艺术家对通过可测量行为归属所有权这一想法的反应与讨论。

Result: 研究发现，量化贡献无法与艺术家对创作意图和能动性的理解相契合；试图通过测量来界定所有权的方式，有可能削弱艺术家理解与实践创作工作的长期假设，暴露了将复杂的社会历史关系转化为可解技术问题的局限性。

Conclusion: 本文批判了将所有权归属简化为技术量化问题的冲动，强调创作所有权是历史与社会情境中的复杂关系，不能仅通过测量行动来解决；应重新审视并尊重艺术家对创作意图与能动性的理解。

Abstract: The integration of AI-driven systems in creative work has sparked debates among artists and legal communities about notions of ownership. Yet there remains little consensus on how ownership should be defined and attributed when human and AI contributions are intertwined. To provoke critical reflection on these tensions, we designed ArtSplit, a provotype that explicitly quantifies human and AI contributions across different stages of creative work. Rather than aiming to resolve ownership, the provotype was used to elicit artists' responses to the idea of attributing ownership through measurable actions in the creative workflow. We argue that quantification fails to align with artists' understandings of creative intent and agency, and that efforts to measure ownership risk diluting long-standing assumptions through which artists understand and practice creative work. This critique challenges the impulse to transform a historically and socially situated relation into a technical problem.

</details>


### [154] [BrainPilot: Automating Brain Discovery with Agentic Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15079)
*Haoxuan Li,Tianci Gao,Jianhe Li,Yang Fan,Runze Shi,Weiran Wang,Tianxiang Zhao,Zezhao Wu,Xiaoyang Jiang,Qihui Zhang,Jia Li,Xiao Xiao,Kai Du,Xiaoxuan Jia,Chao Xie,Lu Mi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了BrainPilot，一个完全开源的多智能体系统，用于加速脑科学研究。系统包含PI智能体协调专业智能体、统一知识库（7,233个索引项）、技能库（72个可复用方法单元）和可审计的追踪图，通过Auditor智能体检查虚假内容，在多项评估中达到与SOTA智能体框架相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑科学研究日益需要跨尺度、跨模态、跨学科整合证据，现有AI智能体缺乏脑科学领域专业知识，存在编造声明、多步推理漂移、缺乏专家干预点等问题。在脑科学领域这些失败代价高昂，因为结论会影响后续科学声明且依赖实验室特定专业知识和谨慎的人类判断。

Method: 提出BrainPilot多智能体系统：1）PI智能体协调基于领域知识的专家智能体；2）构建统一脑科学知识库（7,233索引项）和技能库（72个跨7个研究领域的可复用方法单元）；3）引入追踪图（Graph of Trace）记录主要步骤，链接子目标、工具使用、证据和声明；4）集成Auditor智能体进行编造检查；5）使用开源骨干模型。

Result: 在Agents' Last Exam的三个脑科学任务、自建基准BrainPilotBench-v0以及端到端案例研究中进行评估。BrainPilot使用开源骨干模型达到与最先进智能体框架相当的性能，同时成本更低。

Conclusion: BrainPilot成功实现了可追溯日志和智能体验证结果的脑科学研究加速，通过追踪图提供可审计的工作流记录，解决了现有AI智能体在脑科学领域应用中的关键问题，为脑科学研究提供了有效的AI辅助工具。

Abstract: Understanding the brain increasingly depends on integrating evidence across scales, modalities, and disciplines. Addressing a single research question therefore requires a coordinated sequence of operations, from surveying prior work to executing analyses and interpreting results in light of domain knowledge. AI agents promise to accelerate this process, but current agents lack domain expertise in brain science, may fabricate claims, drift during multi-step reasoning, and offer few defined points for expert intervention. These failures are especially costly in brain science, where conclusions feed into downstream scientific claims and depend on laboratory-specific expertise and careful human judgment. We present \textbf{BrainPilot} a \textbf{fully open-source} multi-agent system that accelerates brain science research with traceable logs and agent-verified results. A principal investigator (PI) agent coordinates specialist agents grounded in curated domain knowledge: a unified brain science knowledge base containing 7{,}233 indexed items and a skill library of 72 reusable methodology units across seven research domains. Every major step is recorded in the Graph of Trace, an auditable record that links subgoals, tool use, evidence, and claims and allows researchers to follow and inspect the workflow. An Auditor agent further integrates fabrication checking into the workflow. For evaluation, we run three brain science tasks from Agents' Last Exam, introduce our own benchmark, \textbf{BrainPilotBench-v0}, and present additional end-to-end case studies. Across these evaluations, BrainPilot with an open-source backbone model attains performance comparable to state-of-the-art agent framework with less costs.

</details>


### [155] [Long-Context Fine-Tuning with Limited VRAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15105)
*Vladimir Fedosov,Aleksandr Sazhin,Artemiy Grinenko,Frank Woernle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HGA结合分段反向传播和分层KV存储，在16GB显存上实现131K tokens的长文本训练与推理，性能媲美全注意力机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调方法虽降低了模型和优化器内存开销，但密集注意力机制仍导致长序列训练成本高昂，显存限制了可处理的上下文长度。

Method: 提出分层全局注意力(HGA)，结合分段反向传播和分层KV存储策略：仅活跃段在VRAM中保持可微分，历史KV解耦至RAM或NVMe，HGA为每个查询块加载有限数量的精确历史token。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上，16GB RTX 5000的密集训练在4K tokens失败，HGA成功扩展至16,384 tokens（峰值15.28GB）。推理时可处理131,072 tokens。2K长度下HGA训练的适配器达到2.7405 nat，与密集训练的2.7383 nat相当，吞吐量略快（217.75 vs 207.02 tokens/s）。

Conclusion: HGA显著扩展了长文本训练的上下文长度边界，在保持与密集注意力相当性能的同时突破了显存限制，随上下文增长吞吐量优势将进一步扩大，为长文本应用提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning reduces model and optimizer memory, but dense attention still makes long training sequences expensive. We combine Hierarchical Global Attention (HGA) with segment-wise backpropagation and tiered KV storage. Only the active segment remains differentiable in VRAM; older KV is detached into RAM or NVMe, and HGA loads a bounded set of exact historical tokens for each query block. On Qwen3-8B with 4-bit QLoRA and PG19, dense training on a 16 GB Quadro RTX 5000 fits 2,048 tokens but fails at 4,096, whereas HGA reaches 16,384 tokens with 15.28 GB peak VRAM. Under evaluation the same adapter runs through 131,072 tokens on this card; VRAM is not constant but grows gently with the resident chunk summaries, so RAM and NVMe capacity set the practical limit beyond these lengths. At the shared 2K training length, HGA-trained and dense-trained adapters obtain 2.7405 and 2.7383 nat under the same dense-attention readout, while the stock model obtains 2.9541. At this boundary HGA training is already marginally faster (217.75 vs. 207.02 tokens/s), and the HGA-to-dense throughput ratio improves from 1K to 2K; because HGA keeps the attended historical set per token approximately constant while dense work per token grows, we expect this lead to widen as context grows. Dense attention is used for the main quality and retrieval comparisons so that they measure the learned weights and remain compatible with standard generation frameworks. HGA can also be used for retrieval and generation; an optimized production-grade serving implementation is under development.

</details>


### [156] [Concept-Guided Spatial Regularization for World Models in Atari Pong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15142)
*Yukuan Lu,Zaishuo Xia,Weyl Lu,Yubei Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文独立评估了五个Atari Pong视觉世界模型，发现均存在轨迹失败和策略性能下降问题，提出CGSReg方法改进关键概念建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型通常作为模型强化学习系统的组件进行评估，而世界模型本身很少被独立研究。作者希望独立评估世界模型的质量，揭示其存在的问题。

Method: 作者复现了五个代表性视觉世界模型（DreamerV3、DIAMOND、TWISTER、Simulus、STORM）的训练流程，冻结训练好的世界模型，通过两种方式评估：（1）闭环展开诊断：用独立训练的策略与冻结模型交互，检查生成的视频轨迹中的视觉和动力学错误；（2）像素空间零样本MBRL：在冻结世界模型内训练新策略后在真实环境中评估。针对发现的问题，提出了概念引导的空间正则化（CGSReg）方法。

Result: 所有五个模型的闭环展开都包含明显的失败，如球消失、球运动错误和无效的球-拍交互。在像素空间零样本MBRL评估中，所有模型的策略性能都显著低于原始MBRL训练管道产生的策略，其中DreamerV3的平均回报从-5.5降至-20.9（接近Pong最低回报-21）。CGSReg方法改进了DreamerV3、DIAMOND和TWISTER的闭环展开和像素空间零样本MBRL性能，但对其他模型和评估指标的效果不同。

Conclusion: 世界模型存在对任务关键概念（如Pong中的球）建模不足的问题。CGSReg作为辅助像素重建损失可以改善部分模型性能，但单一方法无法解决所有世界模型瓶颈，需要更多研究来改进世界模型的建模能力。

Abstract: World models are usually evaluated as components of model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) systems, while the world models themselves are rarely studied in isolation.
  We examine five representative visual world-model agents in Atari Pong: DreamerV3, DIAMOND, TWISTER, Simulus, and STORM. After reproducing their training pipelines and matching the reported agent performance, we freeze the learned world models and evaluate them with a closed-loop rollout diagnostic: a policy trained separately from the corresponding MBRL agent interacts with each frozen model, and the generated video trajectories are inspected for visual and dynamical errors. Across all five models, the rollouts contain clear failures, including ball disappearance, incorrect ball motion, and invalid ball-paddle interactions.
  Beyond visual trajectories, we further evaluate them with pixel-space zero-shot MBRL, where a new policy is trained entirely inside a frozen world model and then evaluated in the real environment. Across all five models, the resulting policies substantially underperform those produced by the corresponding original MBRL training pipelines. The gap is particularly large for DreamerV3, whose mean return drops from -5.5 to -20.9, near the minimum Pong return of -21.
  We hypothesize that insufficient modeling of task-critical concepts, such as the ball in Pong, may contribute to these failures. We therefore propose Concept-Guided Spatial Regularization (CGSReg), an auxiliary pixel reconstruction loss applied to segmented concept regions. Experiments show that CGSReg improves both closed-loop rollouts and pixel-space zero-shot MBRL in DreamerV3, DIAMOND, and TWISTER. Its effects vary across the remaining models and evaluation metrics, indicating that CGSReg alone does not address all world-model bottlenecks.

</details>


### [157] [The Industrialization of Research ; On AI-Driven Science and Its Consequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15164)
*Emmanuel Jeannot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨AI驱动的科学研究所带来的七大结构性挑战，包括科学能力传承的断裂、AI理论的不可解释性、同行评审机制的失效、范式转换能力的质疑、研究议程的外部捕获、系统性错误的积累，以及全球科研社区的分层分化，为负责任地推进AI科学研究提供了条件分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI从科研工具转变为科研过程的自主参与者，科学研究正经历从"手工艺模式"向"流水线模式"的工业化转型。美国能源部的Genesis Mission是这一转型的典型案例。作者旨在审视这一转型背后超出单一项目的根本性问题，探讨AI驱动科学发展的边界条件。

Method: 本文采用思辨性分析方法，系统性地提出并论述了AI驱动科学研究中的七个核心问题：科学能力的代际传承断层、AI生成理论的不透明性、机器生成内容洪流下同行评审的失效、AI范式转换能力的未验证性、科学与政治/工业议程的捕获问题、闭环管道中系统性错误的累积效应、全球科研社区的结构性分化。

Result: 识别出AI驱动科学研究面临的多维度结构性挑战：知识传承机制可能断裂、理论可解释性降低、质量控制体系承压、突破性创新能力存疑、研究自主性受损、错误传播风险增加、以及科研共同体内部可能出现不可通约的层级分化。这些发现为评估AI在科学研究中的合理定位提供了系统性框架。

Conclusion: 上述担忧并非对AI驱动科学研究的否定——其展示的潜力是真实且显著的。这些担忧构成的是负责任地追求该潜力所必须面对的条件和边界。未来的发展需要在拥抱AI潜力的同时，系统性应对这些结构性挑战。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is transforming scientific research -- not merely as a more powerful instrument, but as an autonomous participant in the research cycle itself. This transition constitutes, in the most precise sense of the term, the industrialization of research: a shift from a craft model, in which knowledge, method, and judgment are embedded in the researcher, to a pipeline model, in which these steps are decomposed, automated, and supervised. The US Department of Energy's Genesis Mission is the most ambitious current instantiation of this shift, but the fundamental questions it raises extend far beyond any single program. This essay examines seven such questions: the erosion of the intergenerational transmission of scientific competence; the growing opacity of AI-generated theories; the collapse of peer evaluation under a flood of machine-generated output; the unproven capacity of AI for paradigm-shifting discovery; the capture of the scientific agenda by political and industrial actors; the compounding of systematic errors in closed-loop pipelines; and the structural bifurcation of the global research community into incommensurable tiers. These concerns do not constitute an argument against AI-driven science -- whose demonstrated potential is real and significant. They constitute the conditions under which that potential can be responsibly pursued.

</details>


### [158] [MedFailBench: A Clinician-Built Open-Source Benchmark for Medical AI Safety Boundary Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15166)
*Goktug Ozkan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedFailBench是一个临床医生构建的医学AI安全失败基准测试和错误图集，通过严重程度（1-5级）和安全门类型对医学AI错误进行分类标注，目前发布版本包含44个临床审查案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学AI基准测试主要关注模型是否知道正确答案，但缺乏对安全边界失效情况的系统性评估。需要建立专门针对医学AI安全失败场景的基准测试框架。

Method: 由临床医生构建合成基准测试和错误图集，对医学AI错误按严重程度（1-5级）和安全门类型进行标注，包括：错过紧急转诊、不安全的远程给药、不安全的出院安抚、证据伪造、不安全的方案执行、来源支持缺失等六类安全门。开发自动化流程用于归档模型响应筛选运行。

Result: 当前公开发布版本（v0.2.1）包含44个临床医生审查的合成案例及严重程度标注、HuggingFace实时排行榜预览、安全门分类法、临床严重程度评分规则，以及模型响应筛选运行的自动化归档流程。以Apache-2.0和CC-BY-4.0许可发布，获得Zenodo DOI。

Conclusion: MedFailBench为评估医学AI安全边界失效提供了系统性框架，填补了传统基准测试的空白，为医学AI安全评估提供了新的方法论和工具支持。

Abstract: Most medical AI benchmarks measure whether a model knows the correct answer. MedFailBench asks a different question: which safety boundary failed? We present a clinician-built synthetic benchmark and failure atlas that labels medical AI errors by severity (1--5) and safety gate type (missed urgent escalation, unsafe remote dosing, unsafe discharge reassurance, evidence fabrication, unsafe protocol execution, source support gap). The current public release (v0.2.1) contains 44 clinician-reviewed synthetic cases with severity annotations, a live HuggingFace leaderboard preview, a safety gate taxonomy, a clinical severity rubric, and an automated pipeline for archiving model-response screening runs. No patient data, clinical validation claims, or model rankings are included. MedFailBench is released under Apache-2.0 and CC-BY-4.0 and carries the Zenodo DOI 10.5281/zenodo.21205535.

</details>


### [159] [Benchmarking Multimodal Large Language Models for Scientific Visualization Literacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15176)
*Patrick Phuoc Do,Chau M. Ta,Chaoli Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性评估了6个多模态大语言模型在科学可视化素养测试上的表现，发现Gemini超越人类平均水平，但开源模型表现不佳，揭示了当前模型在SciVis理解能力上的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对多模态大语言模型的评估主要集中在图表类可视化，缺乏对科学可视化理解能力的系统性评估证据。科学可视化作为重要的数据呈现形式，需要专门的能力基准测试来验证模型的实际理解水平。

Method: 使用包含49个测试项的科学可视化素养评估测试（基于18个科学可视化和插图，涵盖8种技术和11种任务类型），在封闭世界协议下评估3个闭源模型和3个开源模型，并与485名人类参与者的数据进行性能对比。

Result: Gemini整体表现最强，在评估子集中超越人类平均水平；开源模型均低于人类基线。模型在不同技术和任务类型上表现不均：在科学插图、搜索和空间理解任务上表现最佳，但在基于纹理和整合的可视化及定量估计任务上表现较差。错误分析揭示了在细粒度定量估计、流向解释和编码解释方面的持续失败。

Conclusion: 当前多模态大语言模型不具备统一的科学可视化素养能力，SciVis素养应成为评估多模态AI系统的必要基准维度，为未来模型改进提供明确的量化参考。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to interpret visualizations, yet current evaluations remain largely chart-centric and provide limited evidence of understanding of scientific visualization (SciVis). We benchmark six MLLMs on the scientific visualization literacy assessment test, a standardized SciVis literacy assessment comprising 49 items based on 18 scientific visualizations and illustrations, spanning 8 techniques and 11 task types. We evaluate three closed-source and three open-source models under a closed-world protocol and compare their performance using data from 485 human participants. Results show that current MLLMs do not exhibit uniform SciVis literacy. Gemini is the strongest model overall, exceeding the human mean across the evaluated subsets, whereas the open-source models remain below the human baseline. Performance is highly uneven across techniques and tasks: models perform best on scientific illustration, search, and spatial understanding, but struggle on texture-based and integration-based visualizations and on quantitative estimation. Error analysis reveals recurring failures in fine-grained quantitative estimation, flow-direction interpretation, and grounded encoding interpretation. These findings position SciVis literacy as a necessary benchmark dimension for evaluating multimodal AI systems. Our code and model outputs are publicly available at https://github.com/patdmp/mllm-scivis-lit-benchmark.

</details>


### [160] [Can We Trust Item Response Theory for AI Evaluation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15190)
*Han Jiang,Sunbeom Kwon,Jinwen Luo,Ziang Xiao,Susu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了项目反应理论(IRT)在AI基准测试中的可靠性，发现经典估计方法在大规模基准测试中不可行，而可扩展估计方法在小规模或非正态分布模型集上可能产生不可靠的推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI基准测试越来越多地使用项目反应理论(IRT)来评估模型能力、排名系统和诊断基准质量，但AI基准数据通常偏离IRT最初开发的人类测试数据模式：基准测试涉及的评估模型更少、项目更多，能力分布可能偏态、聚集或多峰。这种数据模式的不匹配对IRT建模的可靠性提出了挑战，需要系统研究其在AI评估中的适用性。

Method: 研究使用六个广泛使用的LLM基准测试的项目参数和能力分布，在三种常见IRT模型下模拟响应矩阵，比较四种估计工具：边际极大似然估计、马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛、变分推断和神经伪孪生估计器。在18,000个模拟条件下，系统评估计算可行性、可扩展性，以及IRT在模型排名、预测性能和项目特征推断方面的可靠性。

Result: 经典估计器在大规模基准测试设置中变得不可行；可扩展估计器在小规模或非正态分布模型集上可能产生不可靠的项目级和排名推断。研究确定了不同样本大小和诊断方法的可靠性边界，识别了何时潜在特质模型能够可靠支持AI基准测试声明，以及何时可能扭曲评估结论。

Conclusion: 该研究为AI基准测试中IRT方法的可信使用提供了重要指导，明确了所需样本大小和诊断程序，帮助研究者在特定条件下做出合适的方法选择，避免不可靠的评估结论。

Abstract: AI benchmarks increasingly leverage item-level statistical models, particularly item response theory (IRT), to estimate model capabilities, rank systems, select informative examples, and diagnose benchmark quality. However, AI benchmark data often departs from the data regime of human testing, for which standard IRT estimation tools were originally developed: benchmarks typically involve fewer evaluated models, far more items, and capability distributions that may be skewed, clustered, or multimodal. We examine how these regime mismatches challenge the reliability of IRT modeling for AI evaluation. Using item parameters and capability distributions derived from six widely used LLM benchmarks, we simulate response matrices under three common IRT models and compare four estimation tools used in recent benchmark studies: marginal maximum likelihood, Markov chain Monte Carlo, variational inference, and a neural pseudo-Siamese estimator. Across 18,000 simulation conditions, we systematically evaluate computational feasibility, scalability, and the reliability of IRT inferences about model rankings, predicted performance, and item characteristics. Results show that classical estimators can become infeasible in large benchmark settings, whereas scalable estimators can produce unreliable item-level and ranking inferences with small or nonnormally distributed model sets. This study identifies when latent trait models reliably support or risk distorting AI benchmarking claims, and what sample sizes and diagnostics are needed for trustworthy use.

</details>


### [161] [Plover: Steering GUI Agents through Plan-Centric Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15193)
*Madhumitha Venkatesan,Shicheng Wen,Jiajing Guo,Jorge Piazentin Ono,Liu Ren,Dongyu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Plover，一个以计划为中心的视觉GUI自动化系统，通过将任务计划和重计划外化为可检查、可修订的工件，使用户能够监督执行过程并进行局部修正，从而提高了GUI自动化的透明性、可控性和适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉多模态GUI自动化代理在动态布局、意外对话框和界面状态变化等真实环境中容易偏离用户意图，且其规划和适应过程通常是内部的，限制了用户检查、监督或纠正系统行为的能力，因此需要一种能够外化任务计划并支持用户干预的系统。

Method: 采用规划器-执行器架构，将任务计划和重计划作为持久、可检查、可修订的工件进行外化；支持对执行过程的显式监督、通过可编辑计划进行局部修正、自然语言引导以及基于截图的干预，同时在修复过程中保留已有进展。通过形成性研究指导交互设计，并进行基准测试失败案例修复和基于场景的工作流分析来评估系统。

Result: 许多自主GUI代理的失败在计划可见且干预局部化时是结构上可修复的；显式的重规划机制使得GUI自动化更加透明、可控和适应性强。

Conclusion: 通过外化任务计划并支持本地化干预，可以显著改善GUI自动化系统的可修复性和用户体验，证明了计划为中心的方法在提高GUI自动化系统透明度和可控性方面的有效性。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) automation remains challenging in real-world environments, where dynamic layouts, unexpected dialogs, and evolving interface states can cause autonomous agents to drift from user intent. Recent vision-based multimodal agents improve flexibility by operating directly over screenshots and natural language instructions, but planning and adaptation often remain internal, limiting users' ability to inspect, supervise, or correct system behavior. We present Plover, a plan-centric vision-based GUI automation system that externalizes task plans and replanning as persistent, inspectable, and revisable artifacts. Through a planner--executor architecture, Plover supports explicit supervision of evolving execution, localized correction through editable plans, natural-language guidance, and screenshot-grounded interventions, while preserving prior progress during repair. A formative study with six participants informed the interaction design. We then evaluate Plover through benchmark failure-case repair and scenario-based workflow analyses. Our results show that many autonomous GUI-agent failures are structurally repairable when plans remain visible and interventions are localized, and that explicit replanning helps make GUI automation more transparent, controllable, and adaptable.

</details>


### [162] [When Words Are Safe But Actions Kill: Probing Physical Danger Beyond Text Safety in Hidden-State Risk Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15218)
*Weimeng Wang,Ziqiang Wang,Zihang Zhan,Chuanpu Fu,Qi Li,Ke Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现内容危险与物理危险在LLM表示中是可分离信号，提出PRISM探针方法检测物理危险，在多个基准测试上优于同规模LLM判断器，显著降低误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益作为具身智能体的高级规划器，语言上安全的指令在物理世界落地后可能变得危险。需要研究这种物理落地危险是否与普通文本级内容危险相同，以及如何有效检测物理落地危险而非显式不安全措辞。

Method: 通过隐藏状态方向分析和随机分割空值测试，证明内容危险(CD)和物理危险(PD)在LLM表示中形成可分离信号。基于此分离性，提出PRISM——一种基于完整隐藏状态的单层L2正则化逻辑探针。同时构建PhysicalSafetyBench-1K对比基准，包含1000个无直接伤害关键词的物理风险配对。

Result: PRISM在SafeAgentBench上达到86.2-87.7%准确率，误报率11.7-13.7%，而同规模LLM判断器误报率达24.7-39.0%。在PSB-1K上达到99.6%准确率和0.7%误报率，Qwen2.5-3B判断器拒绝67.8%安全任务。在SafeText和EARBench上同样有效。

Conclusion: 内容危险和物理危险是LLM表示中的可分离信号，隐藏状态探针可作为超越文本审核的表示级物理安全方法，PRISM在检测物理落地危险方面显著优于基于LLM的判断方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly serve as high-level planners for embodied agents, where linguistically benign instructions can become unsafe once grounded in the physical world. We study whether this physically grounded danger is the same safety problem as ordinary text-level content danger. Through hidden-state direction analysis and random-split null tests, we show that content danger (CD) and physical danger (PD) form separable signals in LLM representations across Qwen2.5-3B/7B/14B/32B, Phi-3.5 and SmolLM2. Building on the CD/PD separability, we propose PRISM, a single-layer L2-regularized logistic probe over full hidden states. PRISM achieves 86.2--87.7\% accuracy on SafeAgentBench with 11.7--13.7\% FPR, while same-scale LLM judges over-block safe tasks at 24.7--39.0\% FPR. We further introduce PhysicalSafetyBench-1K (PSB-1K), a contrastive benchmark of 1{,}000 physical-risk pairs without direct harm keywords, to test whether methods detect physically grounded danger rather than explicit unsafe wording. On PSB-1K, PRISM reaches 99.6\% accuracy and 0.7\% FPR, whereas a Qwen2.5-3B judge rejects 67.8\% of safe tasks. PRISM also replicates on SafeText and EARBench, supporting hidden-state probing as a representation-level method for physical safety beyond text moderation.

</details>


### [163] [AutoSynthesis: An agentic system for automated meta-analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15247)
*Moein Taherinezhad,Sebastian Maier,Gerardo Vitagliano,Francesco Pierri,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AutoSynthesis，一个端到端的多智能体自动化元分析系统，能够从自然语言研究问题出发，自动完成文献检索、筛选、数据提取和统计分析，生成的结果与专家手工元分析高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证据综合对于将原始研究转化为科学、医学、教育和政策领域的可靠知识至关重要，但定量证据综合目前主要依赖手工操作，难以规模化。这种现状限制了证据综合的效率和适用范围。

Method: 提出了AutoSynthesis系统，这是一个端到端的多智能体自动化元分析系统。系统接收自然语言形式的研究问题，然后自动执行：制定搜索策略、检索科学文献、筛选候选研究、评估全文合格性、提取定量统计数据、计算标准化效应量，最后执行随机效应元分析。系统还支持异质性分析（检验效应量如何在调节变量间变化）以及偏倚风险评估，并生成符合PRISMA指南的透明报告。

Result: AutoSynthesis成功筛选了超过28项研究，提取了20多个定量声明。系统生成的汇总效应估计值与专家手工进行的元分析结果（Hedges' g）高度相似，表明与手动证据综合具有高度一致性。

Conclusion: AutoSynthesis能够使定量证据综合更加可扩展，从而支持跨学科循证决策。该系统为自动化元分析提供了可行的解决方案，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Evidence synthesis is crucial for turning primary research into reliable knowledge for science, medicine, education, and policy. Yet, quantitative evidence synthesis remains largely manual and difficult to scale. Here, we introduce AutoSynthesis, an end-to-end multi-agent system for automated meta-analysis. Given a research question in natural language, AutoSynthesis formulates a search strategy, retrieves scientific literature, screens candidate studies, assesses full-text eligibility, extracts quantitative statistics, computes standardized effect sizes, and finally performs random-effects meta-analysis. AutoSynthesis further supports heterogeneity analysis to examine how effect sizes vary across moderators, as well as risk-of-bias assessment. As output, AutoSynthesis produces a transparent report aligned with PRISMA guidelines. In our application, AutoSynthesis screened over 28 studies and extracted more than 20 quantitative claims. The pooled effect estimates produced by AutoSynthesis are similar to Hedges' $g$ of expert-conducted meta-analyses, indicating close agreement with manual evidence synthesis. Together, these results show that AutoSynthesis can make quantitative evidence synthesis more scalable, thereby supporting evidence-based decision-making across disciplines.

</details>


### [164] [teLLMe Why (Ain't Nothing but a Jam): Exploratory Causal Analysis of Urban Driving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15254)
*Qiwei Li,Jorge Ortiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了teLLMe系统，用于城市驾驶数据集的探索性因果分析。通过结合因果结构学习、PC算法、Bootstrap稳定性检查和DoWhy效应估计，将自然语言问题映射为结构化因果查询，生成包含效应估计、调整集、DAG支持和假设的“因果卡片”，辅助假设生成和专家推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通部门拥有大量视频衍生数据用于安全和拥堵研究，但这些数据大多为观察性数据且无干预，使得如“降雨如何改变交通密度”等因果问题难以回答。

Method: teLLMe系统从行车记录仪标注构建的结构化事件表出发，结合因果结构学习与PC算法、基于Bootstrap的稳定性检查，以及使用线性回归和DoWhy进行查询特定的效应估计。通过模式感知的LLM将自然语言问题映射为结构化因果查询，使用户能够指定处理、结果和子群体。

Result: 在BDD衍生的交通事件案例研究中，系统揭示了涉及天气、高峰时段和交通密度的合理关系，同时明确展示了不确定性和建模选择。

Conclusion: 该系统设计为假设生成和专家推理的工具，而非确定因果结论的来源，旨在帮助研究者探索性分析城市驾驶数据中的因果关系。

Abstract: Traffic agencies now have access to large volumes of video-derived data for studying safety and congestion. Most of these data are observational and collected without interventions, which makes causal questions such as "How would rain change traffic density?" difficult to answer. We present teLLMe, a system for exploratory causal analysis of urban driving datasets. The system starts from a structured event table built from dashcam annotations and combines causal structure learning with the PC algorithm, bootstrap-based stability checks, and query-specific effect estimation using linear regression and DoWhy. Natural-language questions are mapped to structured causal queries through a schema-aware LLM, enabling users to specify treatments, outcomes, and subpopulations. teLLMe returns a "Causal Card" that summarizes effect estimates, adjustment sets, DAG support, and assumptions, followed by a short natural-language explanation. Case studies on BDD-derived traffic events show that the system can surface plausible relationships involving weather, peak hours, and traffic density, while making uncertainty and modeling choices explicit. The system is designed as a tool for hypothesis generation and expert reasoning rather than a source of definitive causal claims.

</details>


### [165] [SearchOS-V1: Towards Robust Open-Domain Information-Seeking Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15257)
*Yuyao Zhang,Junjie Gao,Zhengxian Wu,Jiaming Fan,Jin Zhang,Shihan Ma,Yao Yao,Weiran Qi,Chuyan Jin,Guiyu Ma,Xingzhong Xu,Kai Yang,Ji-Rong Wen,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SearchOS，一个系统级多智能体框架，通过将隐式的搜索进展转化为显式、持久且共享的状态，解决了现有工具集成大语言模型在信息检索任务中因交互历史增长而导致的任务进度追踪困难和搜索循环陷阱问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的工具集成大语言模型虽然具备网络搜索能力，但随着交互历史的增长，智能体难以追踪任务进度。当搜索尝试未能产生有用证据时，当前的单智能体和多智能体系统容易陷入重复循环，浪费搜索预算并最终影响最终输出的质量和完整性。

Method: 1) 将开放域信息检索形式化为带有引证的关系模式补全问题；2) 设计搜索导向的上下文管理(SOCM)系统，将演化状态外化为前沿任务、证据图、覆盖图和失败记忆；3) 应用流水线并行调度机制重叠子智能体执行；4) 引入搜索工具中间件 Harness 来拦截模型和工具交互；5) 提供可重用的分层技能系统。

Result: 在 WideSearch 和 GISA 数据集上的实验结果表明，SearchOS 在所有评估指标上均优于所有评估的单智能体和多智能体基线方法。

Conclusion: SearchOS 成功地将脆弱、隐式的搜索进展转化为显式、持久且共享的状态，通过系统级的多智能体框架实现了鲁棒的信息检索协作，为构建可靠的信息检索系统提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in Tool-Integrated Large Language Models have made web search a core capability of information-seeking agents. However, as interaction histories grow, agents increasingly struggle to track task progress. When search attempts fail to yield useful evidence, current single- and multi-agent systems can become trapped in repetitive loops, wasting search budgets and ultimately compromising the quality and completeness of the final output. We introduce SearchOS, a system-level multi-agent framework that turns fragile, implicit search progress into explicit, persistent, and shared state. First, we formulate open-domain information seeking as relational schema completion with grounded citations, where agents discover entities, populate attributes across linked tables, and anchor each value to source evidence. Then we design Search-Oriented Context Management (SOCM), which externalizes the evolving state into Frontier Task, an Evidence Graph, a Coverage Map, and Failure Memory. Built on SOCM, SearchOS applies a pipeline-parallel scheduling mechanism that overlaps the execution of sub-agents and continuously refills freed slots with tasks targeting unresolved coverage gaps to improve utilization and throughput. To schedule and control the execution of search agents, SearchOS introduces a Search Tool Middleware Harness that intercepts model and tool interactions to record grounded evidence and react to stalls or budget exhaustion, and provides a reusable hierarchical skill system comprising strategy and access skills to augment the agents' search process and avoid repeating failed search patterns across runs. On WideSearch and GISA, SearchOS leads all metrics among the evaluated single- and multi-agent baselines, paving the way toward robust information-seeking collaboration.

</details>


### [166] [Pretraining Data Can Be Poisoned through Computational Propaganda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15267)
*Victoria Graf,Hannaneh Hajishirzi,Noah A. Smith,David Kohlbrenner,Kyle Lo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明了通过公共讨论界面进行网页级内容注入可实现对预训练数据的投毒攻击，并提出了HalfLife分析方法来估计恶意内容被语言模型训练数据包含的程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练数据投毒研究主要针对维基百科等既定数据源，未能反映网页级预训练语料库的大规模和异构特性，且忽视了投毒数据与数据治理管道之间的交互作用。

Method: 利用现有的网页级内容注入机制（公共讨论界面）实施投毒攻击；提出HalfLife这一新颖分析方法，用于估计基于网页爬取的语言模型训练数据中敌对内容的包含情况。

Result: 通过公共讨论界面进行的投毒攻击在网络规模上是可行的；HalfLife分析成功估计了投毒注入是否被包含在预训练数据中；第三方网页内容可作为攻击语言模型预训练的有效载体。

Conclusion: 估计投毒注入是否进入预训练数据具有重要意义；第三方网页内容构成了攻击语言模型预训练的新途径，需要引起安全关注。

Abstract: Poisoning pretraining data can introduce harmful behaviors to LMs that are difficult to detect and mitigate. Prior work on poisoning pretraining data has largely exploited established data sources such as Wikipedia, which do not represent the large scale and heterogeneity typical of pretraining corpora, and has ignored the interaction between poisoned data and data curation pipelines. We demonstrate that poisoning attacks on pretraining data are feasible beyond this limited setting through an existing web-scale content injection mechanism: public discussion interfaces. Additionally, to measure whether malicious content is included after web crawling and data curation, we introduce HalfLife, a novel analysis for estimating adversarial content inclusion in web-crawl based LM training data. We use HalfLife to explore the feasibility of poisoning pretraining corpora at web scale through open discussion interfaces. Our analysis demonstrates the importance of estimating whether poison injections are included in pretraining data, and establishes third-party webpage content as a possible vector for attacking language model pretraining.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [ExaGEMM: Exploration Framework for CPU-Driven ML Inference via Associative In-Register Computing for Low-Bit GEMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14622)
*Hyunwoo Oh,Suyeon Jang,Hanning Chen,Sanggeon Yun,Ryozo Masukawa,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出ExaGEMM框架，通过寄存器驻留的LUT执行机制，协同探索参数化内核与轻量级SIMD指令集支持，在CPU原生低位宽GEMM上实现13.29倍延迟提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低位宽GEMM在高效机器学习推理中日益重要，但极低位宽执行在传统CPU上适配性差。实际部署涉及从1/2/4位权重到不同激活精度的碎片化场景，在固定SIMD和寄存器文件预算下，可行性、复用机会和支持成本各不相同，使得轻量级CPU支持选择成为首要设计问题。

Method: 提出ExaGEMM工作负载感知协同设计框架，核心洞察是现有SIMD数据通路已覆盖表生成和累加，仅需新增寄存器内选择/馈送机制。框架通过寄存器可行性、计算成本、内存流量和硬件开销的解析模型，在仿真前剪枝99.2%的候选空间，随后识别非支配支持点，并生成ISA规范、gem5补丁和GEMM内核进行验证。

Result: 在代表性ML模型和CPU目标上，ExaGEMM相比纯软件基线将延迟提升13.29倍，同时表明工作负载感知的前沿选择对混合精度LLM工作负载尤为重要。

Conclusion: ExaGEMM证明了通过最小化硬件扩展（仅需寄存器内选择/馈送机制）结合工作负载感知的协同探索，可显著提升CPU上低位宽GEMM的性能，为碎片化精度场景提供了系统化的ISA支持选择方法。

Abstract: Low-bit GEMM is increasingly central to efficient ML inference, yet very-low-bit execution remains a poor fit for conventional CPUs. Practical deployment spans fragmented regimes-from 1/2/4-bit weights to varying activation precision-whose feasibility, reuse opportunity, and support cost differ under fixed SIMD and register-file budgets, making lightweight CPU support selection a first-class design problem. We present ExaGEMM, a workload-aware codesign and exploration framework for CPU-native low-bit GEMM via register-resident LUT execution. The key insight is that existing SIMD datapaths already cover table generation and accumulation; the only new hardware is an in-register select/feed mechanism with explicitly modeled cost. ExaGEMM co-explores parameterized kernels and lightweight SIMD ISA support using analytical models of register feasibility, compute cost, memory traffic, and hardware overhead, pruning the candidate space by 99.2% before simulation. It then identifies non-dominated support points and generates ISA specs, gem5 patches, and GEMM kernels for validation. Across representative ML models and CPU targets, ExaGEMM improves latency by 13.29x over software-only baselines, while showing that workload-aware frontier selection is especially important for mixed-precision LLM workloads.

</details>


### [168] [Toward Energy-Efficient and Low-Power Arrhythmia Detection for Wearable Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14747)
*Floriaan Bulten,Yawar Rasheed,Arlene John,Vincenzo Stoico,Ghayoor Gillani*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文通过数据精度降低和近似乘法等近似技术，在保持心律失常分类性能的前提下，实现了深度学习硬件架构的功耗和能耗显著降低（分别降低64.9%和61.5%），有效延长可穿戴设备电池寿命。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管疾病是全球主要死因，心律失常等疾病需要长期监测才能有效诊断。然而现有可穿戴监测设备体积大、舒适性差且依赖临床医生手动评估心电图。虽然深度学习算法在心律失常检测分类方面表现优异，但其计算复杂度高、功耗大，限制了在可穿戴设备上的部署应用。

Method: 在先进深度学习模型及其对应硬件架构中研究近似技术的应用，具体包括数据精度降低和近似乘法两种技术。使用MIT-BIH心律失常数据库对模型进行训练和验证，并综合评估采用各种近似乘法器的硬件实现方案。

Result: 在12 kHz频率下，提出的架构功耗为3.07 μW（能耗2.17 μJ），相比现有8.75 μW（2.08 μJ）的参考架构实现了64.9%的功耗降低，同时保持93.7%的分类准确率和92.1%的灵敏度。在100 MHz频率下，架构功耗为9.45 mW（能耗0.8 μJ），能耗降低61.5%。

Conclusion: 所提出的近似技术显著延长了可穿戴设备的电池寿命，同时保持了所需的心律失常分类性能，证明了近似计算在医疗可穿戴设备深度学习部署中的有效性和实用价值。

Abstract: Cardiovascular diseases are the leading cause of death worldwide, and conditions such as arrhythmia often require long-term monitoring for effective detection and diagnosis. However, current wearable monitoring devices are bulky, uncomfortable, and typically rely on clinicians to manually evaluate electrocardiograms (ECGs). While Deep Learning (DL) algorithms have shown superior performance in arrhythmia detection and classification, their computational complexity coupled with high power consumption limit deployment in wearable devices. To address this challenge, this paper investigates the use of approximation techniques to reduce the power and energy consumption of DL architectures while maintaining acceptable classification performance. Specifically, techniques such as data precision reduction and approximate multiplication are investigated in a state-of-the-art DL model and its corresponding hardware architecture. The model is trained and validated using the MIT-BIH Arrhythmia Database, and hardware implementations employing various approximate multipliers are synthesized and evaluated. Compared with the state-of-the-art 8.75 μW (and 2.08 μJ) reference architecture, our proposed architecture consumes 3.07 μW (and 2.17 μJ) at 12 kHz, showing 64.9% reduction in power consumption while providing an acceptable output quality, i.e., 93.7% classification accuracy and 92.1% sensitivity. At 100 MHz, our proposed architecture consumes 9.45 mW (and 0.8 μJ), showing 61.5% reduction in energy consumption as compared to the state-of-the-art architecture. These results demonstrate that our proposed approximations significantly extend wearable device battery life while preserving the required arrhythmia classification performance.

</details>


### [169] [Valinor: Architectural Support for Fast, Energy-Efficient and Programmable Physical Memory Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14789)
*Konstantinos Kanellopoulos,Spiros Galanopoulos,Konstantinos Sgouras,Vlad-Petru Nitu,Ilias Papalamprou,Andreas Kosmas Kakolyris,Rahul Bera,Dimosthenis Masouros,Dimitrios Soudris,Onur Mutlu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Valinor是一种硬件-操作系统协同的内存分配方案，通过可编程硬件引擎执行操作系统提供的分配库，在保持软件灵活性的同时实现了接近硬件级别的性能，将分配速度提升17倍，端到端性能提升16%，能耗降低8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前系统中，每次小页错误（minor page fault）都会陷入内核并触发流水线刷新、停顿以及一系列分配步骤，开销达数万个时钟周期。对于无服务器函数和微服务等短生命周期工作负载，小页错误可占用高达54%的运行时间和40%的系统能耗。现有硬件分配方案要么牺牲有价值的放置优化，要么依赖固定功能逻辑无法适应新策略或变化的硬件条件。

Method: 提出Valinor，一种硬件-操作系统协同的内存分配基础设施。引入可编程硬件分配引擎，以接近固定硬件的速度执行紧凑的操作系统提供的分配库。支持多种策略，包括短生命周期对象分配器、完整性机制以及硬件遥测引导的放置策略。在运行Linux的BOOM RISC-V软核和全系统模拟器中实现。

Result: 在实际硬件上，Valinor将分配加速17倍，端到端性能提升16%，能耗降低达8%。全系统模拟进一步评估了可编程分配引擎和六个分配库，验证了Valinor在保持可编程性的同时提供硬件级别的性能。

Conclusion: Valinor成功地将软件灵活性与硬件级别性能相结合，为短生命周期工作负载提供了高效的内存分配解决方案，在保持可编程性和策略多样性的同时显著降低了开销，为未来系统设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Physical memory allocation establishes virtual-to-physical mappings on demand. In current systems, each minor page fault traps into the kernel and triggers pipeline flushes, stalls, and a long sequence of allocation steps that can cost tens of thousands of cycles. These overheads are increasingly significant for short-lived workloads such as serverless functions and microservices, where minor faults can account for up to 54% of runtime and up to 40% of system energy. Prior hardware allocation proposals avoid traps and context switches, but either sacrifice useful placement optimizations or rely on fixed-function logic that cannot adapt to new policies or changing hardware conditions.
  We present Valinor, a hardware-OS cooperative memory allocation substrate that combines software flexibility with hardware-class performance. Valinor introduces a programmable hardware allocation engine that executes compact OS-supplied allocation libraries at close to fixed-hardware speed. It supports diverse policies, including short-lived object allocators, integrity mechanisms, and hardware-telemetry-guided placement. We implement Valinor on a BOOM RISC-V soft core running Linux and in a full-system simulator. On real hardware, Valinor accelerates allocation by 17x, improves end-to-end performance by 16%, and reduces energy consumption by up to 8%. Full-system simulation further evaluates the programmable allocation engine and six allocation libraries, showing that Valinor provides hardware-class performance without sacrificing programmability.

</details>


### [170] [CODA: Algorithm-Hardware Co-design for Edge Video Diffusion via NMP-Enabled Compute-Cache Operator Disaggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14908)
*Yuanpeng Zhang,YuXuan Wu,Yitong Xiao,Chenhao Xue,Yi Ren,Cong Li,Yihan Yin,Dimin Niu,Guangyu Sun*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出CODA算法-硬件协同设计架构，通过计算-缓存算子解耦解决边缘设备上视频扩散模型跨时间步缓存（CTC）的内存受限和通信瓶颈问题，实现1.80x加速和1.74x能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型在边缘设备上的部署面临推理速度慢的问题。跨时间步缓存（CTC）虽能减少冗余计算，但在内存受限的边缘GPU上存在两大瓶颈：1)庞大的缓存占用快速超出设备VRAM容量；2)缓存算子与计算算子紧密交织，朴素近存卸载会导致重复的PCIe数据交换，将缓存复用转化为通信和串行受限的执行流。

Method: 提出CODA算法-硬件协同设计架构，核心思想是计算-缓存算子解耦。具体方法包括：1)将密集计算路径和内存受限的缓存路径分离，分别部署在xPU和轻量级DIMM侧近存引擎上；2)将碎片化的缓存活动重组为硬件友好的合并段；3)利用无分类器引导（CFG）分支独立性，实现xPU计算与缓存侧执行的重叠。

Result: 实验结果表明，CODA相比最先进的缓存算法实现了最高1.80x的端到端加速和1.74x的能效提升，同时保持了具有竞争力的生成质量。

Conclusion: CODA通过算法-硬件协同设计和计算-缓存算子解耦，有效解决了边缘设备上视频扩散模型CTC方法的内存和通信瓶颈，为边缘AI部署提供了可行的加速方案。

Abstract: Deploying Video Diffusion Models (VDMs) on edge devices is appealing for localized and privacy-preserving generation, but their iterative Transformer-based denoising remains too slow for practical local inference. Cross-Timestep Caching (CTC) has emerged as a promising direction for reducing redundant computation, reusing activations across adjacent denoising steps rather than modifying model weights, while largely preserving generation fidelity. However, on memory-constrained edge GPUs, CTC requires a massive cache footprint that quickly exceeds on-device VRAM and forces the cache into host memory. More fundamentally, cache operators remain tightly interleaved and chain-dependent with native compute operators, so naive near-memory offloading still incurs repeated PCIe exchanges for residual and fusion computations, turning cache reuse into a communication- and serialization-bound execution flow. We therefore propose CODA, an algorithm-hardware co-designed architecture centered on Compute-Cache Operator Disaggregation. CODA separates dense compute paths and memory-bound cache paths across the xPU and a lightweight DIMM-side near-memory engine, reorganizes fragmented cache activity into hardware-friendly coalesced segments, and exploits Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG) branch independence to overlap xPU compute with cache-side execution. Experiments show that CODA achieves up to 1.80x end-to-end speedup and 1.74x higher energy efficiency, while preserving competitive generation quality compared with a state-of-the-art caching algorithm.

</details>


### [171] [Differentiable Routability-Driven Package Floorplanning with Pin Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15005)
*Yiqi Huang,Zepeng Li,Zhen Zhuang,Kehao Chen,Genggeng Liu,Tsung-Yi Ho*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对先进封装扇出路由的差异化可布线性驱动封装布局与引脚分配算法，在所有测试用例上实现100%可布线性，并相比基线方法减少线长最高约23%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着先进封装技术的发展，重布线层（RDL）中的互连密度不断增加，使得可布线性成为封装布局的关键。同时，电源完整性要求通常为电源分配网络（PDN）预留扇入区域，迫使信号网络通过扇出区域，这使得可布线性估计更加复杂。现有的均匀网格拥塞模型无法准确表征扇出拥塞，而以往的引脚分配方法难以有效评估网络交叉。

Method: 提出三个核心方法：（1）差异化线长最小化方法，直接建模离散芯片取向并通过反向传播线长梯度到芯片位置和取向，在固定引脚选择下减少线长；（2）交叉感知引脚分配方法，将网络交叉成本纳入多策略DPSO算法，并使用GPU并行成本评估；（3）差异化可布线性最大化方法，构建针对扇出路由的拥塞模型，建立从拥塞信息到芯片位置的反向传播路径。

Result: 在所有基准测试上实现100%可布线性。对于基线方法成功布线的测试用例，与配备本文引脚分配流程的领先布局方法相比，线长减少最高约23%。

Conclusion: 所提出的差异化可布线性驱动算法有效解决了先进封装扇出路由中的可布线性问题，通过差异化建模和GPU加速的优化策略，显著提升了布线成功率和线长优化效果，为高密度互连封装设计提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: As advanced packaging technology evolves, increasing interconnect density in redistribution layers (RDLs) makes routability critical to package floorplanning. Meanwhile, power integrity requirements often reserve fan-in regions for the power delivery network (PDN), forcing signal nets through fan-out regions and complicating routability estimation. Existing uniform grid-based congestion models cannot accurately characterize fan-out congestion, while previous pin assignment methods struggle to evaluate net crossings. We propose a differentiable routability-driven floorplanning and pin assignment algorithm for advanced packaging with fan-out routing. First, a differentiable wirelength minimization method directly models discrete chip orientations and back-propagates wirelength gradients to chip locations and orientations. It reduces wirelength under fixed pin selection while avoiding the bias of continuous-angle modeling. Second, a crossing-aware pin assignment method incorporates net-crossing cost into a multi-strategy DPSO algorithm and uses GPU-parallel cost evaluation to reduce wirelength efficiently. Finally, a differentiable routability maximization method constructs a congestion model tailored to fan-out routing and establishes a back-propagation path from congestion information to chip locations, thereby guiding routability optimization. Experimental results show that our method achieves 100% routability on all benchmarks. For cases successfully routed by the baselines, it reduces wirelength by up to approximately 23% compared with a leading floorplanning method equipped with our pin assignment flow.

</details>


### [172] [Pattern-Guided Design Space Exploration for FPGA Accelerator Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15068)
*Jialiang Zhang,Weiman Yan,Yuelin Zou*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出PATTERNDSE框架，通过识别计算模式将HLS设计空间探索的候选方案从140个减少到29个，实现4.83倍整体搜索加速，同时保持最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HLS虽然将FPGA设计抽象到C/C++层次，但高质量的实现仍依赖于流水线、展开、分块、重排序和缓冲等调度决策。这些决策形成了组合爆炸的设计空间，而许多数值核函数展现出重复的计算模式，暗示可以采用不同的优化策略。

Method: 开发PATTERNDSE框架，将重复计算模式（逐元素映射、归约、矩阵-向量运算、矩阵-矩阵运算、模板更新）映射到紧凑的调度空间。应用候选调度，通过LLVM执行验证功能正确性，检查HLS C代码生成，使用模式感知评估器对候选方案排序，最后进行Vitis HLS综合。

Result: 在六个代表性核函数（vecadd、axpy、dot、matvec、gemm、jacobi2d）上评估，与穷举基准相比，将HLS评估候选数从140降至29，实现4.83倍整体搜索缩减，单个核函数最高达12.0倍缩减。所有核函数均恢复到与穷举基准相同的最佳有效Vitis HLS延迟。

Conclusion: 计算模式信息能够有效剪枝非生产性调度组合，同时保持高质量HLS输出，证明了模式引导的设计空间探索方法的有效性和实用价值。

Abstract: High-level synthesis (HLS) raises the abstraction level of FPGA accelerator design from hardware description languages to C/C++, but high-quality results still depend on schedule decisions such as pipelining, unrolling, tiling, reordering, and buffering. These decisions create a combinatorial design space, while many numerical kernels exhibit recurring computation patterns that suggest different optimization strategies. This paper presents PATTERNDSE, a lightweight pattern-guided design space exploration (DSE) framework for FPGA kernels written in Allo, a scheduling-oriented HLS programming system. PATTERNDSE maps recurring computation patterns, including elementwise maps, reductions, matrix-vector operations, matrix-matrix operations, and stencil-like updates, to compact schedule spaces. It then applies candidate schedules, validates functional correctness through LLVM execution, checks HLS C code generation, and uses a simple pattern-aware estimator to rank candidates before Vitis HLS synthesis. We evaluate PATTERNDSE on six representative kernels: vecadd, axpy, dot, matvec, gemm, and jacobi2d. Compared with an exhaustive-lite baseline, pattern-guided DSE reduces the number of HLS-evaluated candidates from 140 to 29, achieving a 4.83x overall search reduction and up to 12.0x reduction for individual kernels. Across all evaluated kernels, PATTERNDSE recovers the same best valid Vitis HLS latency as the exhaustive-lite baseline, demonstrating that computation-pattern information can prune unproductive schedule combinations while preserving high-quality HLS outcomes.

</details>


### [173] [NIFA: Nonlinear IMC enhanced FPGA for efficient ML inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15123)
*Jiajun Hu,Ruthwik Reddy Sunketa,Lei Zhao,Archit Gajjar,Luca Buonanno,Aman Arora*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集成ADC-free IMC模块的新型FPGA架构，通过使用模拟内容寻址存储器(ACAM)替代传统ADC，在CNN和Transformer基准测试中实现了高达40倍和1.9倍的能效提升，以及4.1倍和2.5倍的面积效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于ReRAM的模拟内存计算(IMC)设计仅支持静态权重的向量矩阵乘法(VMM)，将非线性操作和动态矩阵乘法(DIMM)留给FPGA架构处理，导致IMC的优势主要局限于静态权重模型。对于依赖频繁非线性和DIMM操作的Transformer模型，性能提升有限。此外，每个IMC块内的ADC消耗超过70%的面积和功耗，进一步限制了系统效率和可扩展性。

Method: 提出一种新型FPGA架构，集成无ADC的IMC模块，用模拟内容寻址存储器(ACAM)替代传统ADC，在块内原生执行非线性操作。通过FPGA感知的设计空间探索确定最佳交叉开关维度，平衡FPGA面积、灵活性和深度学习性能；开发了利用ACAM执行DIMM操作的高效映射方法，将IMC的适用性扩展到注意力计算。

Result: 在CNN和Transformer基准测试上，该架构实现了高达40倍和1.9倍的更高能效，以及4.1倍和2.5倍的更高面积效率。在基于Transformer的工作负载中，即使面对长输入序列也能保持显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 该架构显著提升了FPGA深度学习推理效率，在Transformer工作负载上保持强劲的性能增益，推动了领域专用FPGA设计的发展，为未来高效深度学习推理提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Recent FPGAs have improved deep learning (DL) inference efficiency through dedicated tensor blocks and in-BRAM computation. ReRAM-based analog in-memory computing (IMC) pushes efficiency further, offering an order-of-magnitude improvement in compute density and energy efficiency over conventional digital logic by performing vector-matrix multiplication (VMM) directly within the ReRAM crossbar; prior work has integrated such IMC blocks into FPGAs for DL inference. However, conventional IMC designs support only static-weight VMM, leaving nonlinear operations and dynamic matrix-matrix multiplication (DIMM) to the FPGA fabric. As a result, the benefits of IMC are largely confined to static-weight models, whereas Transformer-based models, which rely on frequent nonlinear and DIMM operations, gain only limited improvement. Moreover, the ADCs within each IMC block consume more than 70% of its area and power, further limiting system efficiency and scalability. To address these limitations, we propose a novel FPGA architecture that integrates an ADC-free IMC block, replacing the conventional ADC with analog content-addressable memories (ACAMs) that natively perform nonlinear operations inside the block. To fully exploit this block, we conduct an FPGA-aware design-space exploration that determines optimal crossbar dimensions while balancing FPGA area, flexibility, and DL performance, and we develop an efficient mapping that leverages ACAMs to carry out DIMM operations, extending the applicability of IMC to attention computation. On CNN and Transformer-based benchmarks, the proposed architecture achieves up to 40x and 1.9x higher energy efficiency and 4.1x and 2.5x higher area efficiency, respectively. Overall, it significantly improves FPGA DL inference efficiency and sustains robust gains on Transformer-based workloads across long input sequences, advancing domain-specialized FPGA design.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [Transition-Aware Routing in Hybrid Hollow-Core/Single-Mode Fiber Networks: A Cost--Throughput Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14324)
*Md Ghulam Saber,Zhiping Jiang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了混合空心芯光纤/单模光纤网络中的路由权衡问题，发现过度减少光纤转换会导致严重的流量损失，提出了几种折中方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着空心芯光纤（HCF）在现有单模光纤（SMF）网络中的增量部署，出现了一个关键的路由权衡问题：减少HCF-SMF转换可以提高物理层可行性，但过度避免转换会导致有害的路径绕行，需要在转换次数和网络性能之间找到平衡。

Method: 使用通用的事件驱动仿真器，在混合HCF/SMF拓扑上比较六种保护路由方案，涵盖光纤盲选、广义信噪比感知（GSNR-aware）和显式转换感知设计。模型包含每次转换的GSNR惩罚和探索性熔接失败可用性项，在6个参考拓扑、5种HCF部署比例和300爱尔兰动态负载下进行评估。

Result: 最强的转换最小化方案（TPAR和GFJ）将平均转换次数减半，但承载流量损失20-25%；中间设计GMR-T减少约22%转换，吞吐量仅损失3%；BD-TPAR减少约11%转换，成本仅1%。连续HCF部署平均减少40%转换并改善承载流量，降低了激进转换感知路由的收益。

Conclusion: 在碎片化部署下推荐BD-TPAR作为实用的默认方案，GMR-T作为低复杂度替代方案，仅在转换外部成本高时使用TPAR或GFJ；部署模式对性能有重要影响。

Abstract: Incremental deployment of hollow-core fiber (HCF) in single-mode-fiber (SMF) networks introduces a routing tradeoff: reducing HCF-SMF transitions can improve
  physical-layer feasibility, but overly transition-averse routing incurs harmful path detours. We study this tradeoff using a common event-driven simulator
  that compares six protected routing schemes spanning fiber-blind, generalized signal-to-noise ratio (GSNR)-aware, and explicitly transition-aware designs on
  hybrid HCF/SMF topologies. The model includes a per-transition GSNR penalty and an exploratory splice-failure availability term. Across six reference
  topologies, five HCF deployment fractions, and dynamic loads at 300 Erlang, the strongest transition minimizers, transition-penalty-aware routing (TPAR) and
  the GSNR/fiber-transition joint scheme (GFJ), halve the mean transition count at a 20-25% carried-traffic penalty. Among the intermediate designs, GSNR-
  maximal routing with transition-aware reranking (GMR-T) cuts transitions by approximately 22% relative to distance-adaptive routing and spectrum assignment
  (DA-RSA) at a 3% throughput cost, while bounded-detour TPAR (BD-TPAR) cuts transitions by approximately 11% at only a 1% cost. Deployment pattern also
  matters: contiguous HCF rollout lowers transitions by approximately 40% on average while improving carried traffic, reducing the benefit of aggressive
  transition-aware routing. These results support BD-TPAR as a practical default under fragmented deployment, GMR-T as a lower-complexity alternative, and TPAR
  or GFJ only where the external cost of transitions is high.

</details>


### [175] [Assisting Mission-Critical Traffic Flows with Active Queue Management in Industrial Internet of Things](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14478)
*Shuo Wang,Jonathan Kua,Jiong Jin,Yew Wee Wong,Prem Prakash Jayaraman,Zhibo Pang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出在IIoT网络中采用主动队列管理(AQM)机制来缓解缓冲区膨胀问题，实验证明多队列AQM方案能为关键任务流量提供显著的流隔离和性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键任务的工业物联网(IIoT)流量需要严格的网络延迟和抖动保证以确保工业基础设施安全运行。现有网络路由器采用的FIFO缓冲机制会导致缓冲区膨胀现象，而AQM方案在IT领域已证明能改善延迟敏感应用的性能，但在IIoT的OT领域尚未被研究。

Method: 将AQM作为轻量级、非侵入式机制引入IIoT网络，通过实验评估多队列AQM方案在网络压力下对关键任务流量的影响，并与传统FIFO机制进行对比分析。

Result: 实验结果表明，多队列AQM方案能够提供显著的流隔离和容量共享优势，在网络压力下显著改善关键任务流量的性能表现。

Conclusion: AQM机制可作为改善IIoT网络中关键任务流量性能的有效手段，研究基于实验洞察提出了针对性的部署建议。

Abstract: Mission-critical Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) traffic flows require bounded network latency and jitter guarantees to ensure the safe functioning of critical industrial infrastructure. These flows are typically communicated via commodity network routers with conventional First-In-First-Out (FIFO) buffers. FIFO has proven to be the culprit of the well-known bufferbloat phenomenon, and the deployment of Active Queue Management (AQM) schemes have demonstrated significant performance improvements for latency-sensitive applications over the Internet in the IT domain. However, the bufferbloat phenomenon and the efficacy of AQM schemes have not been studied in IIoT-based OT domain. In this paper, we propose the use of AQM as a lightweight and non-intrusive mechanism for assisting mission-critical traffic flows in IIoT networks. Our experimental results demonstrated that multi-queue AQM schemes provide substantial flow isolation and capacity sharing benefits, and significantly improve the performance of mission-critical traffic flows under network pressure. We further provide deployment recommendations based on our experimental insights.

</details>


### [176] [Scheduler-Agnostic Adaptive-FEC for MPQUIC: Field Evaluation over Commercial Cellular Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14482)
*Takuma Tsubaki,Soto Anno,Seiya Komatsu,Takashi Torii,Takuya Tojo*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向MPQUIC Datagram的自适应FEC方案，通过复用QUIC丢包检测信号和现成的RS编码库，在无需修改传输栈或调度器的情况下，显著降低了车载移动环境下的延迟和丢包率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPQUIC Datagram虽适合低延迟应用，但在车载蜂窝网络中极易因丢包而受损；现有自适应FEC或多路径编码方案往往需要对传输栈或调度器做大幅修改，难以实际部署。

Method: 发送端从QUIC丢包信号估计各路径的平滑丢包率，据此动态确定每个FEC块的校验包数量；实现时复用现有的MPQUIC栈与标准Reed-Solomon库，调度器无关。

Result: 在4.0 Mbps主场景、三条商用LTE/5G路径的车载实测中：平均单向延迟从103.0 ms降至70.8 ms，95百分位延迟从281.2 ms降至142.3 ms，丢包率从1.7%降至0.8%，平均编码率0.94。

Conclusion: 调度器无关的自适应FEC可为MPQUIC Datagram在车载移动环境下带来实用的延迟与可靠性增益，且部署友好。

Abstract: Multipath transport has long been studied to improve robustness over heterogeneous networks, and Multipath QUIC (MPQUIC) is particularly attractive for low-latency applications because QUIC Datagram enables transmission without retransmissions. However, this advantage comes with a key drawback: QUIC Datagram is vulnerable to packet loss, especially in vehicular cellular environments with handovers and rapidly varying radio conditions. While prior studies have explored adaptive-FEC and coded multipath designs, practical deployment remains challenging when such mechanisms require substantial modifications to the transport stack or scheduler. In this paper, we present an adaptive Forward Error Correction (FEC) scheme for MPQUIC Datagram that reuses QUIC loss detection signals and an off-the-shelf Reed-Solomon (RS) coding library. The sender estimates smoothed per-path loss rates from QUIC loss signals and adaptively determines the number of parity packets for each FEC block according to the expected packet loss. We implement the proposed scheme on an existing MPQUIC stack and evaluate it through vehicular field experiments over three commercial LTE/5G paths. Under the main 4.0 Mbps setting, the proposed scheme improves both reliability and latency compared with No-FEC: the average one-way delay is reduced from 103.0 ms to 70.8 ms, the 95th-percentile delay from 281.2 ms to 142.3 ms, and the packet loss rate from 1.7 % to 0.8 %, with an average coding rate of 0.94. These results indicate that scheduler-agnostic adaptive-FEC can provide practical latency and reliability gains for MPQUIC Datagram under the measured vehicular mobile conditions.

</details>


### [177] [Formal Verification for Deep Learning-based Power Control in Massive MIMO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14500)
*Thanh Le,Takeshi Matsumura,Yusheng Ji,John C. S. Lui*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文首次提出针对多小区大规模MIMO系统中深度学习功率分配模型的形式化验证框架，在保证±1米位置扰动下的局部鲁棒性同时，将最优性损失控制在1%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于深度学习的无线通信优化方法容易受到输入扰动导致的对抗性攻击影响，而当前的防御机制主要依赖经验方法，缺乏形式化的鲁棒性保证。因此需要建立一个能够提供形式化保证的验证框架来评估深度学习功率分配模型的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出一个形式化验证框架：使用超矩形约束建模对抗者的扰动能力，采用基于抽象的边界传播技术(DeepPoly)来界定潜在分配功率的区间，并将最小性能要求建模为约束规划问题进行数值可行性分析。这是首次在具有非线性输出约束的回归设置中对深度神经网络进行形式化验证。

Result: 在公开的多小区大规模MIMO功率分配数据集上的评估表明，训练良好的模型能够在±1米的位置扰动下保证局部鲁棒性，同时保持最大1%的最优性差距。

Conclusion: 该框架成功填补了深度学习无线通信优化方法缺乏形式化鲁棒性保证的空白，证明了在实际扰动范围内可以同时保证模型的鲁棒性和性能，为深度学习在关键无线通信应用中的部署提供了可靠性保证。

Abstract: Deep learning is a promising approach to optimize wireless communication by simplifying the search for near-optimal solutions. Prior studies on deep learning-based wireless communication optimization have explored supervised learning approaches that map raw user information, such as location or channel state information, to optimal power allocation vectors. While this approach demonstrates competitive performance, it is susceptible to adversarial attacks via input perturbations. Current defense mechanisms primarily rely on empirical methods, which do not provide formal guarantees of robustness. We fill this gap by proposing a formal verification framework to evaluate the robustness of deep learning-based power allocation in multi-cell massive multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems against a wide range of potential adversarial input manipulations. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first attempt to formally verify deep neural networks in a regression setting with non-linear output constraints. We model the adversary's capabilities using hyper-rectangle constraints on their perturbation, adopt the abstraction-based bound-propagation technique (DeepPoly) to bound the interval of potential allocated powers, and formulate the minimum performance requirements as a constrained program for numerical feasibility analysis. Evaluation on publicly available datasets for power allocation in multi-cell massive MIMO indicates that a well-trained model can guarantee the local robustness under location perturbation by +-1m while retaining a maximum 1% optimality gap.

</details>


### [178] [Unified Evaluation Methodology for AI-Native Integrated Sensing and Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14806)
*Filip Lemic,Andra Blaga,Francesco Devoti,Guillermo Encinas Lago,Jan Adler,Amitha Mayya,Padmanava Sen,Giorgos Stratidakis,Sotiris Droulias,Angeliki Alexiou,Alexander Artemenko,Aya Mostafa Ahmed,Visa Koivunen,Robin Rajamäki,Simon Schütze,Robert Elschner,Amélie Hennequart,Ahmad Shoukair,Youssef Nasser,Nahuel Soprano-Loto,François Baccelli,Visa Tapio,Paul Almasan,Andra Lutu,Vincenzo Sciancalepore,Carmen Delgado,Xavier Costa-Pérez*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AI原生ISAC的统一系统架构和评估方法论，包括三阶段验证流程和KPI/KVI报告清单，以弥合理论增益与实际部署性能之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当AI驱动的策略在多种感知任务和目标下在线适应感知和通信时，端到端性能不仅受到波形和信道条件影响，还受到推理延迟、不确定性、环境动态性和硬件非理想性的影响，导致感知精度、通信可靠性和资源开销之间的根本性权衡。现有研究缺乏统一的架构和评估方法来系统地评估这些权衡。

Method: 1) 形式化闭环ISAC的设计空间；2) 提出三阶段验证流程：从界限和可行性分析，到高保真数字孪生仿真，再到初步的空中验证；3) 提供最小报告清单，将技术KPI（如数据速率、SINR、目标检测、参数估计等）与应用级KVI（如可用性和任务有效性）关联；4) 通过UAV室外覆盖扩展和RIS室内覆盖扩展两个实例展示可复现基线的构建方法。

Result: 建立了AI原生ISAC的统一系统架构和评估方法论，提供了从技术指标到应用价值指标的完整映射框架，并通过两个代表性部署场景（UAV室外和RIS室内）演示了如何构建可复现的基线和可比较的证据，帮助弥合理论ISAC增益与部署就绪性能声明之间的差距。

Conclusion: 该工作为AI原生ISAC系统的设计、验证和评估提供了系统化的方法论，使研究者能够在异构部署间建立可复现的基线和可比较的性能证据，推动了ISAC技术从理论分析向实际部署的转化。

Abstract: Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) couples radio sensing, data transmission, and control actions within a single closed-loop system. When Artificial Intelligence (AI)-driven policies adapt sensing and communication online across a variety of sensing tasks and objectives, end-to-end performance is shaped not only by waveform and channel conditions but also by inference latency, uncertainty, environmental dynamics, and hardware non-idealities, leading to fundamental trade-offs between sensing accuracy, communication reliability, and resource overhead. This manuscript presents a unified system architecture and evaluation methodology for AI-native ISAC, defined as ISAC in which learning-based agents adapt sensing, communication, and actuation policies online under uncertainty. We formalize the design space of closed-loop ISAC, propose a three-stage validation pipeline from bounds and feasibility analysis, through high-fidelity digital-twin simulation, to preliminary over-the-air validation, and provide a minimal reporting checklist that links technical Key Performance Indicators (KPIs) (e.g., data rate, SINR, target detection, parameter estimation, track quality, localization error, outage, latency, overhead, and energy per decision) to application-level Key Value Indicators (KVIs) (e.g., availability and mission effectiveness). Two representative instantiations, specifically Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV)-based outdoor and Reconfigurable Intelligent Surface (RIS)-enabled indoor coverage extensions, are used to illustrate how to structure reproducible baselines and comparable evidence across heterogeneous deployments, helping bridge the gap between theoretical ISAC gains and deployment-ready performance claims.

</details>


### [179] [Spoofer or Spoofers? Estimating a Lower Bound on the Number of DRDoS Sources Using Anycast Honeypots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14832)
*Bernhard Degen,Bas Palinckx,Mattijs Jonker,Roland van Rijswijk-Deij,Raffaele Sommese*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种估算伪造流量网络数量下限的新方法，通过部署anycast放大蜜罐，分析了287天的攻击数据，发现至少21.0%的攻击来自多个网络位置，揭示了攻击者经常跨网络分布伪造活动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DDoS攻击仍是重大威胁，特别是分布式反射拒绝服务（DRDoS）攻击难以追溯到其源头。为了更好地理解攻击者行为和部署模式，需要研究攻击源的网络分布情况。

Method: 提出一种估算生成伪造流量的网络数量下限的新方法：1）全球部署anycast放大蜜罐，吸引来自拓扑附近源的请求；2）开发两种估计器，分别基于接收伪造流量的蜜罐集合和观察到的TTL值变化，同时考虑自然路径不稳定性。

Result: 分析287天的放大攻击数据，发现至少21.0%的攻击源自多个网络位置，表明攻击者经常跨网络分布伪造活动，攻击具有分布性特征。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明对抗伪造需要协调的分布式防御措施，并为未来归属技术的设计提供指导，强调了跨网络协同防御的重要性。

Abstract: DDoS attacks remain a significant threat, with distributed reflection denial-of-service (DRDoS) attacks being particularly difficult to trace back to their sources. To better understand attacker behavior and deployment patterns, we present a novel approach for estimating a lower bound on the number of networks involved in generating spoofed traffic. Our approach leverages a global deployment of anycast amplification honeypots that attract requests from topologically nearby sources. Using this infrastructure, we develop two estimators based on the set of honeypots receiving spoofed traffic and on variations in observed TTL values, while accounting for natural path instability. Analyzing 287 days of amplification attacks, we find that at least 21.0% originate from multiple network locations, indicating that attackers frequently distribute spoofing activity across networks. Our findings suggest that combating spoofing requires coordinated and distributed defenses, and inform the design of future attribution techniques.

</details>


### [180] [Periplus: A Resilient In-band SDN Control Plane via Embedded Forwarding Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14869)
*E. M. Castro Barbero,P. de las Heras Quirós,F. J. Simó Reigadas*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出Periplus，一种面向资源受限广域网场景的带内SDN控制平面，实现了自动引导、源路由和快速故障恢复，在Mininet上测试结果显示：故障恢复时间低于50ms，引导过程具备良好可扩展性，且流表规模与网络大小无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源受限的广域电信部署场景需要带内SDN控制平面，但面临自动引导、源路由、快速故障恢复和多控制器协调等关键挑战。

Method: 在引导阶段避免全网泛洪，新交换机加入时控制器仅在两台交换机上安装流规则；路由与故障恢复通过在L2与L3首部间封装图结构（基于NSH的Nicira扩展）编码主路径和逐跳备选路径，交换机沿主路径转发，检测到故障时在本地切换至编码的备选路径，无需控制器参与。

Result: 基于Ryu的实现在Mininet多拓扑测试表明，故障恢复时间低于50ms；引导过程在不同规模与直径拓扑下可扩展；每交换机流表占用与网络规模无关，仅在控制器编码多条备选路径的交换机处增长。

Conclusion: Periplus在资源受限的广域网场景中有效解决了带内SDN控制平面的核心挑战，实现了低延迟故障恢复、可扩展引导与低流表开销，且可运行于标准Open vSwitch。

Abstract: Many resource-constrained, wide-area telecommunications deployments could benefit from an in-band SDN control plane, but several challenges stand in the way. This paper presents Periplus, an in-band SDN control plane designed to address four challenges that this approach presents in such contexts: automatic bootstrapping, source-based routing, fast failure recovery, and multi-controller coordination. The first three are developed in detail, while multi-controller coordination is addressed in a separate paper. For bootstrap, Periplus avoids network-wide flooding: when a new switch joins, the controller installs flow rules in only two switches. For routing and failure recovery, Periplus encodes a primary path and per-hop alternatives in a graph structure encapsulated between L2 and L3 headers; switches forward along the primary path and, upon detecting a failure, fall over locally to the encoded alternative without controller involvement. We evaluate a Ryu-based implementation of Periplus in Mininet across multiple topologies. Periplus runs on stock Open vSwitch (OVS), relying only on its built-in Nicira extensions for Network Service Header (NSH) encapsulation. The evaluation shows sub-50 ms failure recovery, scalable bootstrap across topologies of varying size and diameter, and a per-switch flow-table footprint that is independent of network size and grows only at switches where the controller encodes multiple alternatives.

</details>


### [181] [ANet Patu-1: The Value of Connection in the Agent Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15053)
*Mu Yuan,Jinke Song,Zhaomeng Zhou,Lan Zhang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ANet Patu-1自组织共识协议，使AI代理网络能够自适应地组织协作联盟，证明了异构弱模型群体可通过协作超越同构强模型群体，揭示了协作缩放定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互联网网络价值研究表明不同连接拓扑产生不同价值增长规律（Sarnoff定律V∝N、Metcalfe定律V∝N²、Reed定律V∝2^N），本文旨在探索AI代理网络的类似问题：如何设计协作协议以最大化网络连接价值？

Method: 将网络连接净值建模为协调组规模的函数，推导最优协作协议性质，提出ANet Patu-1自组织共识协议；通过形式化规范和复杂度分析评估协议价值，避免主观评分；使网络能够连续自我重组联盟，在O(1)并行共识轮次内自适应地运行于三种价值增长规律的上包络线上。

Result: （i）涌现效应：异构的廉价模型群体虽初始较弱，但其集体价值随N复合增长并超越同构的强模型群体，形成协作缩放定律而非规模缩放定律；（ii）自反性：异构网络仅给定自身问题而无设计提示，能收敛至ANet Patu-1协议本身，重构支配自身连接价值的高维定律。

Conclusion: AI代理网络的连接价值遵循类似互联网网络的缩放规律，通过自组织共识协议可实现异构弱模型群体对同构强模型群体的超越，证明了协作协议设计的理论基础和实用价值，为AI系统协作提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: The Internet taught us that the value of a network depends on \emph{how} its nodes connect: broadcast stars scale as $V\!\propto\!N$ (Sarnoff), fully-connected meshes as $N^2$ (Metcalfe), and group-forming networks as $2^{N}$ (Reed). We ask the analogous question for networks of AI agents. We model the net value of connection as a function of coordination-group size, derive from it the properties an optimal collaboration protocol must have, and introduce ANet Patu-1 -- a self-organizing consensus protocol in which the network continuously re-forms its own coalitions, adaptively riding the upper envelope of all three regimes at $O(1)$ parallel consensus rounds. To measure value without opinion-grading, we score an emergent protocol by formally specifying it and deriving its complexity, the way distributed algorithms are analyzed. Two results follow. (i)~Emergence -- a crowd of the \emph{cheapest} model, when heterogeneous, starts weak but its collective value compounds with $N$ and \emph{overtakes} a crowd of a far \emph{stronger} model that is homogeneous: a crossover that marks a scaling law for collaboration rather than for scale. (ii)~Reflexivity -- a heterogeneous network, given only its own problem and no design hints, converges on ANet Patu-1 itself, reconstructing the high-dimensional law that governs its own connective value.

</details>


### [182] [Adaptive Sampling for Spatiotemporal Anomaly Monitoring in Wireless Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15235)
*Guoqing Lu,Yixuan Sun,Yiwen Jiang,Bernard Butler*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出哨兵辅助的自适应采样框架，通过KF预测不确定性驱动的稀疏采样和哨兵节点GLR检测的协同工作，显著提高了异常检测窗口的采样率，同时降低了总体成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线传感器网络长期环境监测中，稀疏采样虽然能延长网络寿命，但容易遗漏短暂、局部且可能扩散的异常事件，存在检测盲区问题。

Method: 提出哨兵辅助的自适应采样框架：正常时期采用Kalman滤波(KF)预测不确定性驱动的稀疏采样；异常时期连续采样的哨兵节点执行基于GLR的混合检测，采用节点相对阈值，局部检测触发一跳邻域唤醒及恢复感知的警报控制。

Result: 在Intel Berkeley Research Lab温度数据集上的实验表明，该方法将异常窗口采样率(AWSR)从0.439提升至0.933；相比AAS和Adapted e-Sampling方法，AWSR均有提升，同时总成本分别降低15.4%和2.1%。
